data: {"agent":"specialist-toa","file":"src/utils/audio.ts","content":"import { AudioSettings, PianoKeyItem } from '../types';\n\ninterface ActiveVoice {\n  oscillator: OscillatorNode;\n  gainNode: GainNode;\n  startTime: number;\n}\n\nclass SoundEngine {\n  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;\n  private masterGain: GainNode | null = null;\n  private activeVoices: Map<string, ActiveVoice> = new Map();\n\n  private getAudioContext(): AudioContext {\n    if (!this.ctx) {\n      const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;\n      this.ctx = new AudioCtx();\n\n      this.masterGain = this.ctx.createGain();\n      this.masterGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.7, this.ctx.currentTime);\n      this.masterGain.connect(this.ctx.destination);\n    }\n\n    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') {\n      this.ctx.resume().catch(() => {\n        // ユーザーインタラクション前の resume エラーを無視\n      });\n    }\n\n    return this.ctx;\n  }\n\n  public initAudioContext(): AudioContext | null {\n    return this.getAudioContext();\n  }\n\n  public setMasterVolume(volume: number): void {\n    const ctx = this.getAudioContext();\n    if (!this.masterGain) return;\n\n    const clampedVolume = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, volume));\n    const now = ctx.currentTime;\n    this.masterGain.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);\n    this.masterGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(clampedVolume, now, 0.015);\n  }\n\n  public startNote(key: PianoKeyItem, settings: AudioSettings): void {\n    const ctx = this.getAudioContext();\n    if (!this.masterGain) return;\n\n    // 既に同一キーが鳴っている場合は直ちに解放\n    if (this.activeVoices.has(key.id)) {\n      this.stopNote(key.id, settings, true);\n    }\n\n    const now = ctx.currentTime;\n    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();\n    const noteGain = ctx.createGain();\n\n    // オクターブ計算\n    const octaveMultiplier = Math.pow(2, settings.octaveShift);\n    const targetFreq = key.frequency * octaveMultiplier;\n\n    osc.type = settings.waveform;\n    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(targetFreq, now);\n\n    // アタックおよび初期ディケイのエンベロープ\n    noteGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, now);\n    noteGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.9, now + 0.01);\n    // ピアノ的な減衰（少し減衰してホールド）\n    noteGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.65, now + 0.15);\n\n    osc.connect(noteGain);\n    noteGain.connect(this.masterGain);\n\n    osc.start(now);\n\n    this.activeVoices.set(key.id, {\n      oscillator: osc,\n      gainNode: noteGain,\n      startTime: now,\n    });\n  }\n\n  public stopNote(keyId: string, settings: AudioSettings, immediate = false): void {\n    const voice = this.activeVoices.get(keyId);\n    if (!voice || !this.ctx) return;\n\n    this.activeVoices.delete(keyId);\n\n    const now = this.ctx.currentTime;\n    const { oscillator, gainNode } = voice;\n\n    // サステイン有効時は長いリリース\n    const releaseTime = immediate ? 0.02 : settings.sustain ? 1.0 : 0.12;\n\n    try {\n      gainNode.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);\n      const currentGain = Math.max(gainNode.gain.value, 0.0001);\n      gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(currentGain, now);\n      gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, now + releaseTime);\n\n      oscillator.stop(now + releaseTime + 0.02);\n\n      window.setTimeout(() => {\n        try {\n          oscillator.disconnect();\n          gainNode.disconnect();\n        } catch {\n          // すでに切断されている場合は無視\n        }\n      }, (releaseTime + 0.05) * 1000);\n    } catch {\n      // AudioNodeの操作エラー回避\n    }\n  }\n\n  public stopAllNotes(): void {\n    if (!this.ctx) return;\n\n    const keys = Array.from(this.activeVoices.keys());\n    keys.forEach((keyId) => {\n      this.stopNote(\n        keyId,\n        {\n          volume: 0.7,\n          waveform: 'triangle',\n          octaveShift: 0,\n          sustain: false,\n        },\n        true\n      );\n    });\n  }\n}\n\nconst engine = new SoundEngine();\n\nexport const initAudioContext = (): AudioContext | null => engine.initAudioContext();\nexport const setMasterVolume = (volume: number): void => engine.setMasterVolume(volume);\nexport const startNote = (key: PianoKeyItem, settings: AudioSettings): void => engine.startNote(key, settings);\nexport const stopNote = (keyId: string, settings: AudioSettings): void => engine.stopNote(keyId, settings);\nexport const stopAllNotes = (): void => engine.stopAllNotes();","language":"typescript"}

ControlPanel from './components/ControlPanel';\n\n// C4からC5までの13鍵定義データ（8白鍵と5黒鍵の正確な位置オフセット）\n// 白鍵8鍵（flex: 1 = 各12.5%）、黒鍵の幅 = 7.2%\n// オフセット計算: 中心位置 = (鍵盤の区切り) - (黒鍵幅 7.2% / 2 = 3.6%)\nconst },\n  {\n    id: 'g4',\n    noteNameJp: 'ソ',\n    noteNameEn: 'G4',\n    frequency: 392.0,\n    isBlack: false,\n    keyboardShortcut: 'g',\n  },\n  {\n    id: 'gs4',\n    noteNameJp: 'ソ#',\n    noteNameEn: 'G#4',\n    frequency: 415.3,\n    isBlack: true,\n    keyboardShortcut: 'y',\n    positionOffset: 12.5 * 5 - 3.6, // 58.9%\n  },\n  {\n    id: 'a4',\n    noteNameJp: 'ラ',\n    noteNameEn: 'A4',\n    frequency: 440.0,\n    isBlack: false,\n    keyboardShortcut: 'h',\n  },\n  {\n    id: 'as4',\n    noteNameJp: 'ラ#',\n    noteNameEn: 'A#4',\n    frequency: 466.16,\n    isBlack: true,\n    keyboardShortcut: 'u',\n    positionOffset: 12.5 * 6 - 3.6, // 71.4%\n  },\n  {\n    id: 'b4',\n    noteNameJp: 'シ',\n    noteNameEn: 'B4',\n    frequency: 493.88,\n    isBlack: false,\n    keyboardShortcut: 'j',\n  },\n  {\n    id: 'c5',\n    noteNameJp: '高ド',\n    noteNameEn: 'C5',\n    frequency: 523.25,\n    isBlack: false,\n    keyboardShortcut: 'k',\n  },\n];\n\ninterface PlayedNoteHistory {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  timestamp: number;\n}\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<AudioSettings>({\n    volume: 0.7,\n    waveform: 'triangle',\n    octaveShift: 0,\n    sustain: false,\n  });\n\n  const [labelMode, setLabelMode] = useState<NoteLabelMode>('japanese');\n  const [activeNotes, setActiveNotes] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());\n  const [playedHistory, setPlayedHistory] = useState<PlayedNoteHistory[]>([]);\n\n  // キーボードショートカットの辞書マッピング\n  const shortcutMap = useMemo(() => {\n    const map = new Map<string, PianoKeyItem>();\n    PIANO_KEYS.forEach((key) => {\n      map.set(key.keyboardShortcut.toLowerCase(), key);\n    });\n    return map;\n  }, []);\n\n  // 音量変更をオーディオエンジンへ反映\n  const handleSettingsChange = useCallback((newSettings: AudioSettings) => {\n    setSettings(newSettings);\n    setMasterVolume(newSettings.volume);\n  }, []);\n\n  // 発音開始ハンドラ\n  const handleNoteOn = useCallback(\n    (key: PianoKeyItem) => {\n      initAudioContext();\n      startNote(key, settings);\n\n      setActiveNotes((prev) => {\n        const next = new Set(prev);\n        next.add(key.id);\n        return next;\n      });\n\n      // 演奏履歴を最大8件更新\n      const displayName =\n        labelMode === 'english'\n          ? key.noteNameEn\n          : labelMode === 'keyboard'\n          ? key.keyboardShortcut.toUpperCase()\n          : key.noteNameJp;\n\n      setPlayedHistory((prev) => {\n        const newEntry: PlayedNoteHistory = {\n          id: `${key.id}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`,\n          name: displayName,\n          timestamp: Date.now(),\n        };\n        const updated = [...prev, newEntry];\n        return updated.slice(-8);\n      });\n    },\n    [settings, labelMode]\n  );\n\n  // 消音ハンドラ\n  const handleNoteOff = useCallback(\n    (key: PianoKeyItem) => {\n      stopNote(key.id, settings);\n      setActiveNotes((prev) => {\n        if (!prev.has(key.id)) return prev;\n        const next = new Set(prev);\n        next.delete(key.id);\n        return next;\n      });\n    },\n    [settings]\n  );\n\n  // PC物理キーボードのイベントリッスン\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (e.repeat || e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.altKey) return;\n      const keyChar = e.key.toLowerCase();\n      const targetKey = shortcutMap.get(keyChar);\n\n      if (targetKey) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleNoteOn(targetKey);\n      }\n    };\n\n    const handleKeyUp = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      const keyChar = e.key.toLowerCase();\n      const targetKey = shortcutMap.get(keyChar);\n\n      if (targetKey) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleNoteOff(targetKey);\n      }\n    };\n\n    const handleWindowBlur = () => {\n      stopAllNotes();\n      setActiveNotes(new Set());\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    window.addEventListener('keyup', handleKeyUp);\n    window.addEventListener('blur', handleWindowBlur);\n\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n      window.removeEventListener('keyup', handleKeyUp);\n      window.removeEventListener('blur', handleWindowBlur);\n    };\n  }, [shortcutMap, handleNoteOn, handleNoteOff]);\n\n  return (\n    <main className=\"piano-app\">\n      {/* ヘッダーエリア */}\n      <header className=\"header\">\n        <h1>\n          <span>Web Piano Studio</span>\n          <span GainNode;\n  startTime: number;\n  stopTimer?: number;\n}\n\nexport class SoundEngine {\n  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;\n  private masterGain: GainNode | null = null;\n  private activeVoices: Map<string, ActiveVoice> = new Map();\n\n  private getAudioContext(): AudioContext {\n    if (!this.ctx) {\n      const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;\n      this.ctx = new AudioCtx();\n\n      this.masterGain = this.ctx.createGain();\n      this.masterGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.7, this.ctx.currentTime);\n      this.masterGain.connect(this.ctx.destination);\n    }\n\n    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') {\n      this.ctx.resume().catch(() => {\n        // ユーザーインタラクション前の resume エラーを無視\n      });\n    }\n\n    return this.ctx;\n  }\n\n  public initAudioContext(): AudioContext | null {\n    return this.getAudioContext();\n  }\n\n  public setMasterVolume(volume: number): void {\n    const ctx = this.getAudioContext();\n    if (!this.masterGain) return;\n\n    const clampedVolume = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, volume));\n    const now = ctx.currentTime;\n    this.masterGain.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);\n    this.masterGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(clampedVolume, now, 0.015);\n  }\n\n  public startNote(key: PianoKeyItem, settings: AudioSettings): void {\n    const ctx = this.getAudioContext();\n    if (!this.masterGain) return;\n\n    // 既に同一キーが鳴っている場合は直ちに解放\n    const existingVoice = this.activeVoices.get(key.id);\n    if (existingVoice) {\n      if (existingVoice.stopTimer !== undefined) {\n        window.clearTimeout(existingVoice.stopTimer);\n      }\n      this.stopNote(key.id, settings, true);\n    }\n\n    const now = ctx.currentTime;\n    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();\n    const noteGain = ctx.createGain();\n\n    // オクターブ計算\n    const octaveMultiplier = Math.pow(2, settings.octaveShift);\n    const targetFreq = key.frequency * octaveMultiplier;\n\n    osc.type = settings.waveform;\n    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(targetFreq, now);\n\n    // アタックおよび初期ディケイのエンベロープ\n    noteGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, now);\n    noteGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.9, now + 0.01);\n    // ピアノ的な減衰（少し減衰してホールド）\n    noteGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.65, now + 0.15);\n\n    osc.connect(noteGain);\n    noteGain.connect(this.masterGain);\n\n    osc.start(now);\n\n    this.activeVoices.set(key.id, {\n      oscillator: osc,\n      gainNode: noteGain,\n      startTime: now,\n    });\n  }\n\n  public stopNote(keyId: string, settings: AudioSettings, immediate = false): void {\n    const voice = this.activeVoices.get(keyId);\n    if (!voice || !this.ctx) return;\n\n    if (voice.stopTimer !== undefined) {\n      window.clearTimeout(voice.stopTimer);\n    }\n\n    this.activeVoices.delete(keyId);\n\n    const now = this.ctx.currentTime;\n    const { oscillator, gainNode } = voice;\n\n    // サステイン有効時は長いリリース\n    const releaseTime = immediate ? 0.02 : settings.sustain ? 1.0 : 0.12;\n\n    try {\n      gainNode.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);\n      const currentGain = Math.max(gainNode.gain.value, 0.0001);\n      gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(currentGain, now);\n      gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, now + releaseTime);\n\n      oscillator.stop(now + releaseTime + 0.02);\n\n      const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {\n        try {\n          oscillator.disconnect();\n          gainNode.disconnect();\n        } catch {\n          // すでに切断されている場合は無視\n        }\n      }, (releaseTime + 0.05) * 1000);\n\n      voice.stopTimer = timer;\n    } catch {\n      // AudioNodeの操作エラー回避\n    }\n  }\n\n  public stopAllNotes(): void {\n    if (!this.ctx) return;\n\n    const keys = Array.from(this.activeVoices.keys());\n    keys.forEach((keyId) => {\n      this.stopNote(\n        keyId,\n        {\n          volume: 0.7,\n data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/PianoKeyboard.tsx","content":"import { useRef, useCallback } from 'react';\nimport { PianoKeyItem, NoteLabelMode } from '../types';\n\ninterface PianoKeyboardProps {\n  keys?: PianoKeyItem[];\n  activeNotes?: Set<string>;\n  labelMode?: NoteLabelMode;\n  onNoteOn?: (key: PianoKeyItem) => void;\n  onNoteOff?: (key: PianoKeyItem) => void;\n}\n\nconst PianoKeyboard = ({\n  keys = [],\n  activeNotes = new Set(),\n  labelMode = 'japanese',\n  onNoteOn,\n  onNoteOff,\n}: PianoKeyboardProps) => {\n  const activePointerIdsRef = useRef<Set<number>>(new Set());\n  const pointerKeyMapRef = useRef<Map<number, string>>(new Map());\n\n  const whiteKeys = keys.filter((k) => !k.isBlack);\n  const blackKeys = keys.filter((k) => k.isBlack);\n\n  const handlePointerDown = useCallback(\n    (key: PianoKeyItem, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n      activePointerIdsRef.current.add(e.pointerId);\n      pointerKeyMapRef.current.set(e.pointerId, key.id);\n\n      // 要素のポインタキャプチャを明示的に解放し、指やマウスのスライド移動で他キーへ遷移できるようにする\n      try {\n        if (e.currentTarget.hasPointerCapture(e.pointerId)) {\n          e.currentTarget.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);\n        }\n      } catch {\n        // キャプチャ操作の非互換ブラウザ例外を防止\n      }\n      onNoteOn?.(key);\n    },\n    [onNoteOn]\n  );\n\n  const handlePointerUp = useCallback(\n    (key: PianoKeyItem, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n      activePointerIdsRef.current.delete(e.pointerId);\n      pointerKeyMapRef.current.delete(e.pointerId);\n      onNoteOff?.(key);\n    },\n    [onNoteOff]\n  );\n\n  const handlePointerEnter = useCallback(\n    (key: PianoKeyItem, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n      if (e.buttons === 1 || activePointerIdsRef.current.has(e.pointerId)) {\n        activePointerIdsRef.current.add(e.pointerId);\n        pointerKeyMapRef.current.set(e.pointerId, key.id);\n        onNoteOn?.(key);\n      }\n    },\n    [onNoteOn]\n  );\n\n  const handlePointerLeave = useCallback(\n    (key: PianoKeyItem, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n      if (pointerKeyMapRef.current.get(e.pointerId) === key.id) {\n        pointerKeyMapRef.current.delete(e.pointerId);\n        onNoteOff?.(key);\n      }\n    },\n    [onNoteOff]\n  );\n\n  const handlePointerCancel = useCallback(\n    (key: PianoKeyItem, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n      activePointerIdsRef.current.delete(e.pointerId);\n      pointerKeyMapRef.current.delete(e.pointerId);\n      onNoteOff?.(key);\n    },\n    [onNoteOff]\n  );\n\n  const getLabelContent = (key: PianoKeyItem): string => {\n    switch (labelMode) {\n      case 'japanese':\n        return key.noteNameJp;\n      case 'english':\n        return key.noteNameEn;\n      case 'keyboard':\n        return key.keyboardShortcut.toUpperCase();\n      case 'none':\n      default:\n        return '';\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className=\"keyboard-wrapper\"\n      onPointerUp={(e) => {\n        activePointerIdsRef.current.delete(e.pointerId);\n        pointerKeyMapRef.current.delete(e.pointerId);\n      }}\n    >\n      <div className=\"keyboard-container\">\n        {/* 白鍵群のレンダリング */}\n        {whiteKeys.map((key) => {\n          const isActive = activeNotes.has(key.id);\n          const activeClass = isActive ? ' key-active' : '';\n          const label = getLabelContent(key);\n\n          return (\n            <button\n              key={key.id}\n              type=\"button\"\n              className={`key-white${activeClass}`}\n              aria-pressed={isActive}\n              onPointerDown={(e) => handlePointerDown(key, e)}\n              onPointerUp={(e) => handlePointerUp(key, e)}\n              onPointerEnter={(e) => handlePointerEnter(key, e)}\n              onPointerLeave={(e) => handlePointerLeave(key, e)}\n              onPointerCancel={(e) => handlePointerCancel(key, e)}\n              aria-label={`${key.noteNameJp} (${key.noteNameEn})`}\n            >\n              {labelMode !== 'none' && <span className=\"key-label\">{label}</span>}\n              {labelMode !== 'keyboard' && (\n                <span className=\"key-shortcut\">{key.keyboardShortcut.toUpperCase()}</span>\n              )}\n            </button>\n          );\n        })}\n\n        {/* 黒鍵群の絶対配置レンダリング */}\n        {blackKeys.map((key) => {\n          const isActive = activeNotes.has(key.id);\n          const activeClass = isActive ? ' key-active' : '';\n          const label = getLabelContent(key);\n          const leftStyle = key.positionOffset !== undefined ? `${key.positionOffset}%` : '0%';\n\n          return (\n            <button\n              key={key.id}\n              type=\"button\"\n              className={`key-black${activeClass}`}\n              style={{ left: leftStyle }}\n              aria-pressed={isActive}\n              onPointerDown={(e) => handlePointerDown(key, e)}\n              onPointerUp={(e) => handlePointerUp(key, e)}\n              onPointerEnter={(e) => handlePointerEnter(key, e)}\n              onPointerLeave={(e) => handlePointerLeave(key, e)}\n              onPointerCancel={(e) => handlePointerCancel(key, e)}\n              aria-label={`${key.noteNameJp} (${key.noteNameEn})`}\n            >\n              {labelMode !== 'none' && <span className=\"key-label\">{label}</span>}\n              {labelMode !== 'keyboard' && (\n                <span className=\"key-shortcut\">{key.keyboardShortcut.toUpperCase()}</span>\n              )}\n            </button>\n          );\n        })}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default PianoKeyboard;","language":"tsx"}

data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/ControlPanel.tsx","content":"import { ChangeEvent } from 'react';\nimport { AudioSettings, NoteLabelMode, WaveformType } from '../types';\n\ninterface ControlPanelProps {\n  settings: AudioSettings;\n  labelMode: NoteLabelMode;\n  onSettingsChange: (settings: AudioSettings) => void;\n  onLabelModeChange: (mode: NoteLabelMode) => void;\n}\n\nconst ControlPanel = ({\n  settings,\n  labelMode,\n  onSettingsChange,\n  onLabelModeChange,\n}: ControlPanelProps) => {\n  const handleWaveformChange = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => {\n    onSettingsChange({\n      ...settings,\n      waveform: e.target.value as WaveformType,\n    });\n  };\n\n  const handleVolumeChange = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {\n    onSettingsChange({\n      ...settings,\n      volume: parseFloat(e.target.value),\n    });\n  };\n\n  const handleLabelModeChange = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => {\n    onLabelModeChange(e.target.value as NoteLabelMode);\n  };\n\n  const handleOctaveShift = (delta: number) => {\n    const nextOctave = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, settings.octaveShift + delta));\n    onSettingsChange({\n      ...settings,\n      octaveShift: nextOctave,\n    });\n  };\n\n  const handleSustainToggle = () => {\n    onSettingsChange({\n      ...settings,\n      sustain: !settings.sustain,\n    });\n  };\n\n  const getOctaveLabel = (shift: number): string => {\n    if (shift > 0) return `+${shift}`;\n    if (shift < 0) return `${shift}`;\n    return '標準';\n  };\n\n  const sustainActiveClass = settings.sustain ? ' toggle-btn-active' : '';\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"controls-container\">\n      {/* 音色選択 */}\n      <div className=\"control-group\">\n        <label htmlFor=\"waveform-select\" className=\"control-label\">\n          音色 (波形)\n        </label>\n        <select\n          id=\"waveform-select\"\n          className=\"select-input\"\n          value={settings.waveform}\n          onChange={handleWaveformChange}\n        >\n          <option value=\"triangle\">ピアノ風 (三角波)</option>\n          <option value=\"sine\">まろやか (サイン波)</option>\n          <option value=\"square\">8-Bitゲーム (矩形波)</option>\n          <option value=\"sawtooth\">ブラス・リード (ノコギリ波)</option>\n        </select>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* 音名表示モード選択 */}\n      <div className=\"control-group\">\n        <label htmlFor=\"label-mode-select\" className=\"control-label\">\n          鍵盤ラベル表示\n        </label>\n        <select\n          id=\"label-mode-select\"\n          className=\"select-input\"\n          value={labelMode}\n          onChange={handleLabelModeChange}\n        >\n          <option value=\"japanese\">ドレミ (日本語)</option>\n          <option value=\"english\">C D E (英語)</option>\n          <option value=\"keyboard\">PCキーのみ</option>\n          <option value=\"none\">非表示</option>\n        </select>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* オクターブ変更 */}\n      <div className=\"control-group\">\n        <span className=\"control-label\">オクターブ</span>\n        <div className=\"octave-controls\">\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className=\"octave-btn\"\n            onClick={() => handleOctaveShift(-1)}\n            disabled={settings.octaveShift <= -1}\n            aria-label=\"オクターブ下げる\"\n          >\n            -\n          </button>\n          <span aria-live=\"polite\">{getOctaveLabel(settings.octaveShift)}</span>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className=\"octave-btn\"\n            onClick={() => handleOctaveShift(1)}\n            disabled={settings.octaveShift >= 1}\n            aria-label=\"オクターブ上げる\"\n          >\n            +\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* 音量調整 */}\n      <div className=\"control-group\">\n        <label htmlFor=\"volume-slider\" className=\"control-label\">\n          <span>音量</span>\n          <span>{Math.round(settings.volume * 100)}%</span>\n        </label>\n        <div className=\"volume-icon\">\n          <input\n            id=\"volume-slider\"\n            type=\"range\"\n            min=\"0\"\n            max=\"1\"\n            step=\"0.01\"\n            className=\"range-input\"\n            value={settings.volume}\n            onChange={handleVolumeChange}\n            aria-label=\"音量スライダー\"\n            aria-valuemin={0}\n            aria-valuemax={100}\n            aria-valuenow={Math.round(settings.volume * 100)}\n 'k',\n  },\n];\n\nconst generateId = (): string => {\n  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && typeof crypto.randomUUID === 'function') {\n    return crypto.randomUUID();\n  }\n  return `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)}`;\n};\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<AudioSettings>({\n    volume: 0.7,\n    waveform: 'triangle',\n    octaveShift: 0,\n    sustain: false,\n  });\n\n  const [labelMode, setLabelMode] = useState<NoteLabelMode>('japanese');\n  const [activeNotes, setActiveNotes] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());\n  const [playedHistory, setPlayedHistory] = useState<PlayedNoteHistory[]>([]);\n\n  // キーボードショートカットの辞書マッピング\n  const shortcutMap = useMemo(() => {\n    const map = new Map<string, PianoKeyItem>();\n useCallback((newSettings: AudioSettings) => {\n    setSettings(newSettings);\n    setMasterVolume(newSettings.volume);\n  }, []);\n\n  // 発音開始ハンドラ\n  const handleNoteOn = useCallback(\n    (key: PianoKeyItem) => {\n      initAudioContext();\n      startNote(key, settings);\n\n      setActiveNotes((prev) => {\n        const next = new Set(prev);\n        next.add(key.id);\n        return next;\n      });\n\n      // 演奏履歴を最大8件更新\n      const displayName =\n        labelMode === 'english'\n          ? key.noteNameEn\n          : labelMode === 'keyboard'\n          ? key.keyboardShortcut.toUpperCase()\n          : key.noteNameJp;\n\n      setPlayedHistory((prev) => {\n        const newEntry: PlayedNoteHistory = {\n          id: generateId(),\n          name: displayName,\n          timestamp: Date.now(),\n        };\n        const updated = [...prev, newEntry];\n        return updated.slice(-8);\n      });\n    },\n    [settings, labelMode]\n  );\n\n  // 消音ハンドラ\n  const handleNoteOff = useCallback(\n    (key: PianoKeyItem) => {\n      stopNote(key.id, settings);\n      setActiveNotes((prev) => {\n        if (!prev.has(key.id)) return prev;\n        const next = new Set(prev);\n        next.delete(key.id);\n        return next;\n      });\n    },\n    [settings]\n  );\n\n  // PC物理キーボードのイベントリッスン\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (e.repeat || e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.altKey) return;\n      const keyChar = e.key.toLowerCase();\n      const targetKey = shortcutMap.get(keyChar);\n\n      if (targetKey) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleNoteOn(targetKey);\n      }\n    };\n\n    const handleKeyUp = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      const keyChar = e.key.toLowerCase();\n      const targetKey = shortcutMap.get(keyChar);\n\n      if (targetKey) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleNoteOff(targetKey);\n      }\n    };\n\n    const handleWindowBlur = () => {\n      stopAllNotes();\n      setActiveNotes(new Set());\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    window.addEventListener('keyup', handleKeyUp);\n 'sawtooth';\n\nexport type NoteLabelMode = 'japanese' | 'english' | 'keyboard' | 'none';\n\nexport interface PianoKeyItem {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly noteNameJp: string;\n  readonly noteNameEn: string;\n  readonly frequency: number;\n  readonly isBlack: boolean;\n  readonly keyboardShortcut: string;\n  readonly positionOffset?: number;\n}\n\nexport interface AudioSettings {\n  volume: number;\n  waveform: WaveformType;\n  octaveShift: number;\n  sustain: boolean;\n}\n\nexport interface PlayedNoteHistory {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  timestamp: number;\n}","language":"typescript"},{"path":"src/utils/audio.ts","content":"import { AudioSettings, PianoKeyItem } from '../types';\n\ninterface ActiveVoice {\n  oscillator: OscillatorNode;\n  gainNode: GainNode;\n  startTime: number;\n  stopTimer?: number;\n}\n\nexport class SoundEngine {\n  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;\n  private masterGain: GainNode | null = null;\n  private activeVoices: Map<string, ActiveVoice> = new Map();\n\n  private getAudioContext(): AudioContext {\n    if (!this.ctx) {\n      const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;\n      this.ctx = new AudioCtx();\n\n      this.masterGain = this.ctx.createGain();\n      this.masterGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.7, this.ctx.currentTime);\n      this.masterGain.connect(this.ctx.destination);\n    }\n\n    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') {\n      this.ctx.resume().catch(() => {\n        // ユーザーインタラクション前の resume エラーを無視\n      });\n    }\n\n    return this.ctx;\n  }\n\n  public initAudioContext(): AudioContext | null {\n    return this.getAudioContext();\n  }\n\n  public setMasterVolume(volume: number): void {\n    const ctx = this.getAudioContext();\n    if (!this.masterGain) return;\n\n    const clampedVolume = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, volume));\n    const now = ctx.currentTime;\n    this.masterGain.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);\n    this.masterGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(clampedVolume, now, 0.015);\n  }\n\n  public startNote(key: PianoKeyItem, settings: AudioSettings): void {\n    const ctx = this.getAudioContext();\n    if (!this.masterGain) return;\n\n    // 既に同一キーが鳴っている場合は直ちに解放\n    const existingVoice = this.activeVoices.get(key.id);\n    if (existingVoice) {\n      if (existingVoice.stopTimer !== undefined) {\n        window.clearTimeout(existingVoice.stopTimer);\n      }\n      this.stopNote(key.id, settings, true);\n    }\n\n    const now = ctx.currentTime;\n    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();\n    const noteGain = ctx.createGain();\n\n    // オクターブ計算\n    const octaveMultiplier = Math.pow(2, settings.octaveShift);\n    const targetFreq = key.frequency * octaveMultiplier;\n\n    osc.type = settings.waveform;\n    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(targetFreq, now);\n\n    // アタックおよび初期ディケイのエンベロープ\n    noteGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, now);\n    noteGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.9, now + 0.01);\n    // ピアノ的な減衰（少し減衰してホールド）\n    noteGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.65, now + 0.15);\n\n    osc.connect(noteGain);\n    noteGain.connect(this.masterGain);\n\n    osc.start(now);\n\n    this.activeVoices.set(key.id, {\n      oscillator: osc,\n      gainNode: noteGain,\n      startTime: now,\n    });\n  }\n\n  public stopNote(keyId: string, settings: AudioSettings, immediate = false): void {\n    const voice = this.activeVoices.get(keyId);\n    if (!voice || !this.ctx) return;\n\n    if (voice.stopTimer !== undefined) {\n      window.clearTimeout(voice.stopTimer);\n    }\n\n    this.activeVoices.delete(keyId);\n\n    const now = this.ctx.currentTime;\n    const { oscillator, gainNode } = voice;\n\n    // サステイン有効時は長いリリース\n    const releaseTime = immediate ? 0.02 : settings.sustain ? 1.0 : 0.12;\n\n    try {\n      gainNode.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);\n      const currentGain = + releaseTime + 0.02);\n\n      const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {\n        try {\n          oscillator.disconnect();\n          gainNode.disconnect();\n        } catch {\n          //
